# Goldito Feature Status and Test Guide

> **Who is this for?** People building the app together (Minsik · Seulgi · Muk) and people who test it.
> **What does it tell you?** ① What is built now and what is not yet ② How to check it ③ The results of checking.
> **When is it updated?** Every time one task is done, fix the status table and the scenarios **in the same commit** ([CLAUDE.md](../../CLAUDE.md) §5).
> Product flow: [the README, How Goldito works](../../README.md#how-goldito-works). Task queue: [GitHub issues](https://github.com/minsikpaul92/Goldito/issues).

**Last updated:** 2026-10-09 · #60 merged (fixes FLOW-13 to 23 · REPORT-11 to 12 · RV-6 to 10) · tests run on **main = Vercel** (§1.1) · Seulgi's QA track [Phase Q](phases/phase-q-quality.md) · Earlier: first manual test results (§3.0), code review scenarios (§3.17)

---

## 1. Getting started

### 1.1 Access: always test main on Vercel (decided 2026-10-09)

| Environment | Address | Notes |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Production (main): hand testing happens here** | **https://goldito-petcare.vercel.app** | Deploys automatically every time main is merged. Record results (✅) only for tests done here |
| Vercel preview (optional) | The Preview link in the PR comment | For a look before merging; results are recorded against main |
| Local (only while developing) | `http://localhost:8081` + backend `http://localhost:8000` | `cd frontend && npx expo start --web --port 8081`, `cd backend && .venv/bin/uvicorn app.main:app --port 8000` |

> **Backend (U0, 2026-10-09):** The Vercel build points at Render's **https://goldito-backend.onrender.com** → uploads · AI · demo reset (Profile → Demo tools) work on Vercel. It is a free plan, so it **sleeps after 15 minutes with no requests, and the first request then takes 30 to 50 seconds** (a keep-alive cron keeps it awake). PR **Previews are not in CORS**, so uploads · AI do not work there. Record results on main (Production).
> Every environment connects to the **hosted Supabase (the real DB)**. This means tests create real data (see 1.4).

### 1.2 Demo accounts

On the sign-in screen, **Try the demo** → the **Demo owner** / **Demo sitter** buttons (no password needed).

| Account | Name | Has |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `demo-owner@goldito.test` | Robert (owner) | Dog **Max**, cat **Mochi** |
| `demo-sitter@goldito.test` | Chloe (sitter) | 1 confirmed booking with Robert (below) |

**Sets for team QA (#163):** one set per person, 1 owner + 1 sitter. Pick a set in the **Whose demo** dropdown on the sign-in screen to sign in as that set's Demo owner / Demo sitter. Profile → Demo tools resets **only the signed-in account** (an owner resets the owner side, a sitter the sitter side, other accounts stay as they are). It shows only in builds with demo tools on; the judging build shows only the **Judge 1 to 4** seats (#411, 3.62): owner `demo-owner-judgeN@goldito.test`, sitter `demo-sitter-judgeN@goldito.test` (Robert and Chloe, pets Max and Mochi), one browser per seat.

| Set | Owner | Sitter |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Chloe (default, Seulgi's QA) | `demo-owner@goldito.test` (Robert) | `demo-sitter@goldito.test` (Chloe) |
| Paul | `demo-owner-paul@goldito.test` (Peter) | `demo-sitter-paul@goldito.test` (Paul) |
| Allen | `demo-owner-allen@goldito.test` (Alex) | `demo-sitter-allen@goldito.test` (Allen) |
| Daniel | `demo-owner-daniel@goldito.test` (David) | `demo-sitter-daniel@goldito.test` (Daniel) |

> **Names:** all eight account names are different, and pet names differ per set too (Chloe set Max · Mochi, Paul set Biscuit · Luna, Allen set Cooper · Olive, Daniel set Teddy · Leo). The dog in every set has a chicken allergy, so the Treat check sample result is the same.
>
> **What a reset clears:** when the owner resets, the owner side (pets and care plans, Treat check Recent checks, the owner's notifications; a pet the owner added is removed in every state, so the set's own pets are what is left) and **the bookings · inquiries · chats with that set's sitter** are deleted; the sitter's schedule and tone stay. When the sitter resets, the sitter side (schedule, the sitter's notifications) and the same bookings · inquiries · chats are deleted; the owner's pets and care plans stay. Bookings made with accounts of another set are not deleted by either reset.
>
> **What a reset creates again (10/20):** in every state except `empty`: 1 favorite for the owner (that set's sitter), one chat for that pair (the Stay card + a short conversation, plus the request card and the sitter's answer if the state has a booking), the sitter's onboarding answers (if answers already exist they are left as they are; only when there are none: walks · meals · at home · activity · park · dog size · cats) and the onboarding-done mark. The seed script also adds the sitter answers. Photos (they need a Cloudinary upload) are not there yet. The owner is marked as having finished the sign-up questions (`onboarded_at`), and Max · Mochi get personality · safety answers, so the "at a glance" card shows right away (after `014v` is applied).
>
> **Where sets meet:** every demo sitter shows up in every owner's search. Bookings between sets (e.g. the Paul owner and the Chloe sitter) are not deleted by any set's reset. The new sets (Paul · Allen · Daniel) get pets and schedules from the first reset (Pets only) after the seed is run.

> **Demo emails:** hosted DB accounts are `@goldito.test` (names Robert / Chloe). Do not run the old `seed_demo.py` (`@pawddy.test`) on the hosted DB.

### 1.3 Demo data (as of 2026-10-04; for 2026-10-08 see below)

- Robert ↔ Chloe booking **confirmed**, Meet & Greet done, **before payment**. Max · Mochi.
- Drop-off done (10/4), **pick-up planned 10/7 05:37 UTC (Toronto 10/7 1:37 AM)**.
- So **right now Chloe is "in care"** → the tasks · check-ins on the sitter Home show.
- ⚠️ **Once the pick-up time has passed**, the sitter-side scenarios (TASK · CHK) are blocked with "Tasks open once the stay has started" and similar. Then make a new booking and mark the drop-off done, or use the seed script (Phase 10, 10.1).
- **The state depends on who did what last.** To reset to the start state you want (`empty` · `pets` · `confirmed` · `ready` · `in_care`), see test-run.md §1 (`backend/scripts/reset_demo.py`, dry run first, on Minsik's PC; after the backend is deployed, Profile → Demo tools in the app).

### 1.4 Data that tests leave behind

| Can delete (in the app) | Cannot delete (intended rule) |
| :--- | :--- |
| Care tasks (Delete), feed photos (🗑️) | Check-ins, completion records, notifications: read-only for the client |

→ Notifications · check-ins pile up after testing. Reset with `reset_demo.py` / Demo tools if needed. For judging, Phase 10 switches to fixed demo accounts.

## 3. Scenarios

**Columns:** Auto: whether an automated test checks the same scenario (spec name). **Status**: ✅ passed · ❌ failed · ➖ not checked (+ date · who checked). A row no one has checked by hand yet is **➖**.

**Legend** ✅ checked by a person and passed · 🟡 partly (or checked by Claude, waiting for a human check) · ❌ checked by a person and failed · ➖ not tried yet. The table below shows only what **a person saw directly** in this first test (runbook §2, steps 1 to 5).

A person needs to check again |

Before Returned · a pet not in the stay · another sitter · the owner cannot write; an empty review (no chip and no note) is refused; nobody can delete. The pet's owner and other sitters can read; other owners · logged-out users cannot | SQL smoke `015u` | Once after `015u` is applied to the hosted DB |
| DONE-17 | API: `POST /api/ai/pet-review-chips` (#407) | Only the booking's sitter (403 for the owner), only after the pick-up (409 `stay_not_finished` before), 404 for a pet not in the stay. Up to 6 chips: Nemotron chips (from the stay's check-in totals · this Life Record, in the sitter's first person; chips over 40 characters are dropped) → chips made straight from the check-ins ("Ate well every day", "Loved our walks" (dogs only) and so on) → 2 common chips. If the model fails, 200 with the check-in chips (`source: stay`). Only this sitter's records of this stay are used. Nothing is saved | pytest `test_ai_pet_review` | Once with the real model (chips must not invent things the stay lacks) |
| DONE-18 | Sitter: on a finished booking ("Stay complete 🐾" after Returned), under the medal, **How was Max?** → **Review Max** for each pet | After "Finding chips…", chips taken from the stay (common chips like "A joy to look after" if the AI is down). Save is disabled with no chip and no note. The note is hidden behind **+ Add a note** (≤ 200 characters). Save → toast "Review saved. Robert can see it", and the card becomes "Your review of Max" (chosen chips · note · "Robert and other sitters can see this."). **Edit** keeps it one review. Before Returned there is no card | 🤖 `pet-review` | Once with the demo accounts after finishing a stay with two pets |
| DONE-19 | Owner: Home → Pet page, below the pet room (below the medal) | That pet's reviews, newest first ("Chloe · Oct 6 to Oct 9", chosen chips · note). Nothing shows when there are no reviews. Switching pets shows only that pet's reviews. Read only | 🤖 `pet-review` | Once with the demo owner |
| DONE-20 | Another sitter: the pet card (below the collapsed Life Record) on a request · booking detail that includes the pet | Reviews from other stays, newest first (the sitter's name is the first word). My own review of the booking being viewed does not repeat in the pet card (only in the My review card below) | 🤖 `pet-review` | Once with another demo sitter account |

### 3.18 Pet Transit (TRIP): Phase 06B · *DB (S1) only for now; screen scenarios are added below as each step is done*

| ID | Step | Expected result | Auto | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| TRIP-1 | Start trip before payment · earlier than 2 hours before the agreed time · by the person who is not traveling (the sitter, if at the sitter's place) | All refused (`not_paid` · `trip_too_early` · `not_traveler`); a third party gets `not_allowed` | SQL `rls_smoke` L | ➖ |
| TRIP-2 | Owner starts the trip with a first location → a few location updates → within 150 m of the destination | `en_route` + distance · ETA; duplicate calls within 1 second are ignored; two good positions in a row within 150 m, `arrived` and one notification to each side (`015q`, TRIP-25); no location is saved after that (the last point on the map = the position that decided the arrival) | SQL `rls_smoke` L · 015q | ➖ |
| TRIP-3 | End trip · Cancel trip | Location · ETA · distance are cleared, `completed` / `cancelled`; a new Start trip is possible for the same booking · same handoff | SQL `rls_smoke` L | ➖ |
| TRIP-4 | An account not on the booking; coordinates in the sitter profile list | 0 `trips` rows; reading the sitter's `home_lat` column is refused; a direct client INSERT · UPDATE is refused | SQL `rls_smoke` L | ➖ |
| TRIP-5 | Handoff place = owner's home / somewhere else | The sitter travels (destination = the owner's home coordinates) / either may travel but there is no destination → no map · ETA, arrival is not judged by distance | SQL `rls_smoke` L | ➖ |
| TRIP-6 | Sitter uploads a handoff photo and asks for a photo check (dog photo / room with no animal / in a car with no crate) | `ok` if a dog is visible and the species matches; `warning` if there is no animal or the species differs; a car photo is `ok` only if a crate or seatbelt shows (the server rules win even if the model says "looks safe"). If the model fails or takes over 20 seconds, it is still saved as 200 `unchecked`. 403 if not the booking's sitter or someone else's photo | 🤖 pytest `test_ai_handoff_check` · SQL `rls_smoke` L (row read · write rights) · **crate detection in a car waits for a check with a real photo 👤** | ➖ |
| TRIP-7 | Profile → **Home address** card → **📍 Use my current location** | Location is not read before the button is tapped (no pin status · neighborhood · map link · purpose copy). One tap opens the browser permission prompt (or goes straight on if already allowed) → toast **"Location found 📍 Tap Save to keep it"**; **Home address** gets "street, city" (e.g. Dundas St W, Toronto) and **Postal code** separately gets the first 3 characters of the postal code (e.g. M6J) (the person adds the house number). Save stores it with the pin and toasts **"Pin saved near {neighborhood}"**. If the browser refuses, under the field: "Location is off. Allow it in your browser settings, or check your address instead." The pin stays | 🤖 `home-location.spec.ts` + pytest `test_geo` (reverse) · real phone 👤 | ➖ |
| TRIP-8 | Type a new address or edit it, then Save | A one-line toast "Profile saved · Pin placed near {neighborhood, city}". If the address is not found or the lookup is down: "We couldn't place that address on the map. Use your current location for the pin." and the old pin is cleared. If the address is untouched, no lookup and the pin stays. A pin set from the current location in the same visit is not overwritten by the address lookup | 🤖 `home-location.spec.ts` + pytest `test_geo` | ➖ |
| TRIP-9 | Route of the simulated drive (Simulate the drive) | Start = the traveler's home; each step gets closer to the destination; the last point is about 60 m before the destination (inside the 150 m arrival rule). Arrival needs two positions inside 150 m (`015q`), so it stays one more step at the last point. Real GPS positions are sent with their accuracy (m) | 🤖 `location.spec.ts` | ➖ |
| TRIP-10 | From 2 hours before the handoff, owner taps **Start trip** on the booking detail → consent screen → **Simulate the drive (demo)** | The consent screen comes first and no trip is made before it. Once started, the Trip screen: "Sharing your location with Chloe until you arrive" banner, "ETA 7:42 AM · 3.2 km", map (🚗 · 📍, ± buttons, Tiles © Esri). After about 30 seconds "You've arrived 🚗", the banner goes away and **Chloe's place** shows (address · Visitor parking · lobby directions) | 🤖 `transit.spec.ts` | ➖ |
| TRIP-11 | **Cancel** (or Close) on the consent screen | Neither location sharing nor the trip starts; stays on the booking detail | 🤖 `transit.spec.ts` | ➖ |
| TRIP-12 | The side that is not traveling (the sitter, if at the sitter's place) | No Start trip. An owner earlier than 2 hours before sees only one line, "You can start the trip from 7:00 AM" | 🤖 `transit.spec.ts` | ➖ |
| TRIP-13 | Sitter on Home: "Robert is on the way · ETA …" card → Trip screen, then the owner arrives | Map + ETA (with distance), no my-location banner · Cancel trip. On arrival, a "Robert has arrived" card without a refresh (polling within 4 seconds). When the trip ends the Home card goes away. ### 3.34 Same-day stay quote (QUOTE) · #172

`quote_booking` returns a same-day stay (drop-off · pick-up on the same day) as `nights: 0`, `days: 1` and charges one day. The card then shows days instead of nights, and `nights` is not passed to the AI draft.

| ID | Try | Expected result | Auto | Person |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| QUOTE-1 | Request Boarding for one day (e.g. Oct 21, 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM) → quote card | **"1 day × $55.00"**, total $55.00. No "0 nights" | 🤖 `checkout` | ➖ |
| QUOTE-2 | Again with 1 night · 2 nights | "1 night × …", "2 nights × …", unit price × count = base amount | 🤖 `checkout` (3 nights) | ➖ |
| QUOTE-3 | AI draft for a same-day inquiry | Says only the total and never "0 nights" (the model gets only `days`) | pytest `test_inquiry` · `test_inquiry_agent` · **real model 👤** | ➖ |

| ID | Steps | Expected result | Auto | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DECL-1 | Owner → **Decline** next to **I'd like to book** in the chat footer → reason chip (optional) · note (optional) → **Decline and end the request** | A "Not this sitter?" sheet; confirming shows the toast "Request ended", the booking is Cancelled (reason saved), the sitter is notified, and the date hold is released. The footer goes back to **Request booking** | 🤖 `chat` | ➖ |

Confirming makes the booking Cancelled, with no Pay button. The final quote card on the sitter's screen has no Decline | 🤖 `chat` | ➖ |

(+ note) goes to the chat. Price · booking status stay as they are (the price is computed by the server). | TOASTPOS-3 | Checkout · sign-up questions (screens with pinned buttons) | Right above the pinned button area, not covering the buttons | 🤖 `checkout` (all pass) | **👤** position |
| TOASTPOS-4 | Screens that don't report a pinned area (chat, etc.) | Keeps the old height (raised by the tab bar height), so it doesn't cover hand-drawn button rows | **👤** | ➖ |

> **Meet the pet (#367):** opens Add pet's sign-up questions (`/owner-onboarding?add=1`, Dog outlined on the first screen): voice, photo, breed and coat, then breed needs with sources. Tavily searches live only for a breed outside the reviewed list (for example Shiba Inu, Jindo).

## 4. Known limits (check before filing a bug)

- **AI button sparkle icon (#404):** Buttons that ask the AI to write or check (Diary Write/Rewrite the page, chat reply chips, Treat check, voice Use this, care Make a checklist) have a line-icon sparkle (`Icon name="ai"`) before the label. The ✨ emoji is nowhere. Waiting for a person to check.

- **Automatic arrival needs two good positions (TRIP-25, #381):** The browser gives a new value only when the position changes, so if the phone stands still within 150 m, the second position may come late or never. Then finish with **I've arrived** on the chat card. A position with accuracy worse than 100 m (indoor Wi-Fi · IP location) shows on the map but is not used for arrival or "about 5 minutes away". `015q` has to be applied to the hosted DB when the PR merges, and before that, sending a GPS position (`p_accuracy`) fails on the hosted DB (the simulated drive sends no accuracy and works as before).

- **Two-finger sticker size (DIARYTAP, #369):** **both fingers must be on the sticker** (with one finger on the paper, it only drags). For a small sticker, it is easier to make it bigger with the corner handle first and then use two fingers. There are no size · rotate · delete buttons (#369), so the sticker size can't be changed with the keyboard alone.

- **Feed delete · hide (FEEDX, #345):** a photo the sitter sent to the owner can be deleted for both **only within 30 minutes of sending** (by the server clock; the app shows the button by the same 30 minutes). After that, deleting is on my side only, and it stays in the other side's Feed. Hiding can be undone (Hidden at the bottom of Feed); deleting can't. The photo file is deleted only when nobody can see it anymore. A sitter whose care has ended can keep reading only their own posts (photos the owner shared, only until the end of the care period), and new photos they post are only-me. `015j` was applied on the hosted DB on 2026-10-24 (the old app code still works as is).

- **Update lines are for the Meet & Greet only (MGUPD):** the agreement (I'd like to book · I accept) · payment cards change in place as before, with no new line. The chat list preview is "Meet & Greet update" for any change (the list only knows the card type).

- **The signature name is checked only in the app (PAYWHY-3):** the Pay button turns on only when it matches the profile name, but the server (`sign_booking_consent`) only checks that the name is 1-80 characters. A call that doesn't go through the app can sign with a different name. It is a demo payment, so the server check wasn't added. Also, the gray Pay still takes taps (it scrolls to what is missing), so screen readers don't read it as "disabled"; the reason line right above it says why instead.

- **A Meet & Greet the owner picked first has no time limit (MGCHOICE):** the 2-day · 3-day reminders and auto-cancel (011l) count from the sitter's first agreement. If the owner sends a time or asks to skip and the sitter doesn't answer, the request stays as it is until the sitter answers or one of them cancels.

- **Daily limits (audit 2026-10-21):** AI calls and upload signing are 100 a day (UTC) per person, 1000 for the shared demo accounts. The counts live only in backend memory, so they start from 0 again when Render restarts. If a test hits the limit, wait until the next day or restart Render.

- **Demo account passwords · emails (`014z`):** only the seed (`scripts.seed_demo`) can change them. To change them in the SQL Editor or the dashboard, first run `select set_demo_accounts_unlocked(true);` (5 minutes).

- **Search doesn't filter by service · handoff method yet (CHAT-10):** the booking request sheet has no service picker (the service the sitter offers; Boarding if they offer both). Filtering search by the owner's · sitter's onboarding choices (service, who brings the pet) comes once there is onboarding data. When both would fit, the sitter who gets the request accepts · changes · declines it.
- **Payment is a demo, and holding and paying out the money is display only (CHAT-22):** "Held until the stay ends" is just wording; no money actually moves. Paying the sitter after care ends is handled in #146 (end · review). The DB doesn't block payment when there is no final quote card (the old booking screen · demo seed), but it does block it while a sent final quote is waiting for the owner's answer (`quote_pending`, 014y). The chat screen shows Pay only once the final quote is confirmed. If the time changes after confirming, the fees on that booking's final quote (the quote confirmed after the payment card) stay in the price. But the chat's Pay button shows the quote card's old base price, so it may differ from the new total in Checkout. Sending a quote card is the sitter's button, and the AI never sends one by itself (action chips are the later part of #142).
- **The chat AI only suggests (CHAT-11 to 13, D62):** there is no auto-send. There are reply chips and action chips (CHAT-24), and even action chips do nothing until tapped. Logs of suggestion acceptance (tapped or dismissed) aren't kept yet (the chips · drafts that were made are in the `chat_ai` log, CHAT-26). The sitter's first reply has no amount: the price goes out with **Send quote** (CHAT-25). For bookings with no Meet & Greet card in the chat (old bookings), the action chip that opens that card opens nothing. The old inquiry screens and their AI drafts are still there (merging them into the chat is the next PR).
- **The chat doesn't send notifications yet (CHAT):** a new message shows only through the unread count in the list and the open chat updating (the bell doesn't ring). The existing inquiry screens and the sitter's AI draft flow are unchanged, and messages from new inquiries are not copied into the chat (merged in #142). Existing inquiries are moved into threads only once, when `014j` is applied.
- **Text built in the DB still has dashes (COPY, #173):** notification titles · bodies (`005` · `006` · `009d` · `010` · `011l` · `012`), task done captions (`008`), `fmt_date_range` (`003`). Only the app code was fixed; the SQL needs a new migration, so it is handled separately.

- **Sitter search distance is straight-line and shown rounded to whole km (SEARCH):** the radius is applied exactly (5 km means within 5 km). Sitter coordinates are never sent down, but an owner who searches many times with changing coordinates could narrow the sitter's home down to about 1 km. For the hackathon demo (anonymous seed) this is left as is, and distance bucketing · rate limits are noted under TODO Later.

- **The photo request button shows only by the app's "in care" rule (drop-off time to pick-up time, `isCaring`) (11.1):** the server (`request_photo`) accepts requests from 30 minutes before the drop-off, but during those 30 minutes Feed doesn't show the button yet.
- **Known issues from the 2026-10-15 Snap review (removed; in git history): already recorded, no need to file separate issues (P0 SR-1 to SR-4 are fixed; the ones below are Later):** ✨ Decorate → Save before the stickers load (or after a failure) can delete saved stickers (SR-5) · in a Mood video, if the pet boxes are caught differently from frame to frame, pets can get mixed up (SR-6) · a new owner who adds a pet from a Snap tool and comes back still sees "Add a pet first" (SR-8) · the sitter Diary doesn't show stickers the owner newly added until a refresh (SR-16).

- **Entry info is only a warning and doesn't block payment (ENTRY-1):** at checkout for house sitting · a handoff at the owner's home, if the entry info form is empty, a card says so and gives a link, but the owner can ignore it and Pay (e.g. when family opens the door). Then the sitter sees "{owner} hasn't added entry info yet, message them." instead of Show code. Adding entry info to the demo seed is in the Phase 10 seed plan (it isn't in the seed now).
- **Treat check sample labels are made-up images** (`frontend/assets/demo/labels/` = `backend/tests/fixtures/labels/`, fake product names with no brand · a real ingredient label format). A person can swap in real photographed labels (same file names). The progress text "Checking for {name}…" changes by **time** (8 seconds), not by server step. "Recent checks" in the Snap tab shows only **the last 3** (with names if there are several pets; all 10 are inside the Treat check screen).
- **Mood percentages are a rule-based calculation for fun:** the vision model only picks ear · eye · mouth · tail · posture · tension · action tags, and the table in `backend/app/ai/mood_rules.py` makes the %. So the same photo → the same %, but if a tag is misread, the % is wrong too. Toys aren't caught well by the mouth shape options, so **a `toy` tag is asked for separately** (of our 10 photos, only the tug photo was `in_mouth`, 2026-10-14), and a toy in the mouth counts as "play", so **excited** comes first. It is not a model validated for emotions (D61, "For fun only").
- **Mood is web only**; the pet finder (MediaPipe `tasks-vision@1.0.1`) is downloaded from jsdelivr · Google storage when it runs (WASM 11 MB + model 4.5 MB, a few seconds on first use). If the network is blocked, "Couldn't get the pet finder ready…". Video frames need a codec the browser can open (mp4 in desktop Chrome · Safari is OK). Results aren't saved.
- **Sticker cut-out takes about 5 seconds each** (Magic Touch, CPU; the GPU gives an empty mask, so it isn't used). It runs in a classic Web Worker, so the screen doesn't freeze (browsers without workers use the main thread, and the screen freezes briefly meanwhile). The 30 MB model file comes from Google storage and is cached for 1 hour. A leash · harness may be cut out along with the pet, and a pet touching the photo's edge is cut off at that edge. Deleting a sticker leaves it in diaries already decorated (decoration items carry the image `public_id` too; the Cloudinary file isn't deleted).
- **Diary decorating uses coordinates (0 to 1) on the "paper" that holds the daily report text:** the owner and sitter see the same paper (same text · same size), so the same spot, but if the screen widths differ a lot (a wide window outside the phone frame), the text wraps differently and the sticker's position relative to the text can shift a little. Up to 20 stickers per person's layer. Decorating is web only (app builds don't draw stickers).
- **Save image (11.8) redraws the paper the browser is showing onto a canvas:** text with the screen's line breaks · font as is, stickers at their saved position · size · angle. Photos · stickers are loaded again with CORS (Cloudinary allows it), so any that fail to load are left out and a toast says how many. Decorating in progress (unsaved) isn't saved: tap **Save stickers** first. Web only; the file is a PNG at 2x the on-screen paper size.
- **The diary public link (DB.5) really is public:** it is a Cloudinary image URL, so anyone with the link can open it, and it can't be deleted from the app (link deletion comes after the hackathon). So the sheet says so before uploading. The phone share sheet (Share image) shows only when the browser supports file sharing (desktop Chrome · Firefox usually don't → Download / link).
- **The picture diary handwriting font (Gaegu) comes from Google Fonts** (web only): if it is blocked, the system font shows, and if the font arrives late, a different font shows briefly at first. **The 9 default Goldito stickers are placeholders drawn in code** (`frontend/assets/stickers/`; replace them with design files under the same names). The picture box photo is the first shared photo of the day, so if there are private photos the owner can't see, the pictures on the two screens may differ (the box size is the same, so sticker positions match).

- **The owner Diary tab shows only the daily reports the sitter sent:** live updates (Live) are on Home, and the full record is in History. There is no Live section in the Diary yet (the IA follow-up in TODO).
- **Inquiry AI**: if Render is asleep, the first reply takes 30 to 50 seconds longer. The auto-send delay (about 30 seconds) is a temporary formula (until Seulgi confirms it). Owners can go back into past inquiries from "Your questions" in the Bookings tab.
- **"Can't" answers are not auto-sent (RV-2):** if the dates don't work (or can't be checked), the draft waits for the sitter's check even for an auto-send sitter; the server blocks it even if the model says "no check needed". The tool-using agent mode doesn't accept an answer that didn't look at the calendar (`check_availability`), and rewrites it through the single-call path.
- **Inquiry AI availability = the booking engine's rules (RV-1):** if even one slot lacks room for the number of pets, or the sitter hasn't opened even one of the days, it is "can't". When the sitter checks again with Regenerate · Accept / Decline · Suggest, it also judges **by the owner who asked** (`012d`, CR-3) (`012d`, applied on the hosted DB 10/13). **Before `011c` is applied** on the hosted DB, this check can't run, so every reply comes out as "I'll check and let you know" (no price, not auto-sent).
- **AI answers to follow-up questions (FB-34, known limit):** the model reads the latest owner message + the earlier conversation (up to 6), but **dates · quote · availability are based on the original schedule saved in the inquiry**. So even to a follow-up like "Could you open for me?" it may answer "no room that day"; in that case the sitter should open the schedule or write the reply themselves (a prompt that marks an owner's request to open the schedule as needing the sitter's check belongs to Q.4b / RV-2).
- **Change dates and 011j · 011k:** the owner's Change dates needs `011j`, and the sitter's Decline · Suggest (can_host=false, no quote) needs `011k` applied on the hosted DB (without them, "Couldn't change the dates / send the reply"). The new app fails to send replies on the old DB, and the old app (Vercel main) still works after 011k (`p_outcome` is optional). The sitter's **Suggest** message is plain text, so there is no button yet for the owner to "switch to these dates" in one tap (they pick them with Change dates).
- **Booking stages (011l):** migration `011l` **ships in the same PR** as the app (if applied first, the old app's first-meeting proposals get blocked with `interest_required`). The sitter's default is **one owner at a time**, so to take two owners on the same dates the sitter has to turn on the setting in their profile (to start with it on for the test account Chloe, turn it on in Profile). **Holds apply only to requests the sitter agreed to**: requests before agreement don't hold dates, so two owners' requests for the same dates can show together, and once one is held, the other's agreement fails. The sitter calendar the owner sees (free days / full days) **doesn't reflect other owners' holds yet** (it says `sitter_unavailable` the moment the request is sent). The sweep is a cron that runs every 15 minutes, so the 2-day · 3-day rules apply up to 15 minutes late. A request before the first agreement only shows its stage as `requested`, and the owner's decline reason (chip) shows on the sitter's screen only as a cancel reason, not in the notification body.
- **Decline note length (FB-35):** the 200-character limit is only on the app's input field (the server `respond_booking` doesn't check the length). The reason chip and note are saved joined into one sentence (`sitter_note` · notification body), so the owner screen doesn't show the chip and the note separately.
- **Writing back on an inquiry (FB-34):** the owner's **Write back** field shows *only when the sitter's reply is the last message* (after I send, it is hidden until the sitter replies), and not when the inquiry is `booked` · `closed`. The backend makes a new draft from "new owner messages since the last draft"; if Render is asleep or the model fails, the draft is late or missing, and the sitter can reply directly with **Write it myself**. **Change dates** only makes a new inquiry and doesn't close the old one. An inquiry whose place was *Somewhere else* doesn't save its note, so the place note must be written again in the sheet.
- **Records after an early Received (CW-1, fixed 2026-10-09):** check-ins · tasks · daily report chips · drafts all count from "the **earlier** of the agreed drop-off and Received" (the end stays the agreed pick-up). The check-in · task side is a DB function, so it changes only **after `011i` is applied on the hosted DB**; before that, for 3 minutes after an `in_care` reset, check-ins are blocked with "Tasks open once the stay has started" (the daily report chips were fixed by the backend deploy alone).
- **Care request form limits (Phase 06 decision):** no weekly repeats like "a week" (tasks repeat daily or happen once). The count style ("3 times a day") isn't a counter but automatic time placement (8 AM · 2 PM · 8 PM, editable in the table). A counter-request's extra cost is only recorded and shown (no extra payment). During care, one open request per pet (pending or counter). During care the owner can still edit · delete existing tasks directly; only adding is a request. Requests aren't validated when sent but are created when approved, so a request that breaks the rules (length · species) can error on approval.
- **No server push (6.7):** no alarms while the app is closed, and alarm pop-ups only while the app is open (sound · vibration when the browser allows it).
- **How the AI daily report avoids hallucination:** the model gets only one `source_snapshot`, the model isn't called when there are no records, and rule breaks (gendered pronouns · "on time") get one rewrite. When the prompt changes, rerun `backend/scripts/check_daily_report.py` and have a person read the output.
- History is **read only**. The sitter Diary (writing daily reports) came in 7.3 (stack #55).
- Heads-up shows on the sitter **booking detail and Home**. The "arrival card" (Pet Transit) is built in Phase 06B.
- History merges 3 kinds of records (tasks · check-ins · feed photos) on screen. A photo that is also in Feed shows only once.
- Check-ins **can't be undone after sending** (there is only a double-tap lock). If needed, add a "10-second undo".
- Deleting a notification keeps it in History (notifications = to clear, records = to keep).
- AI features: inquiry replies (07B) · daily report (07) · caption (09) · Life Record (07C) are merged into main (#55 to #58, fixes #60). The safety check (08) isn't done yet. Known issues are in the 2026-10-08 code review (removed; in git history).
- The sample photo tray shows only in the **desktop frame / demo accounts**. On a real phone + a regular account, **Take photo / Choose from library**. The photo samples are the same set (dogs · cats · daily life) in every photo picker except labels · video · daily report pictures. Cat photos are in `frontend/assets/demo/mood/` = `backend/tests/fixtures/mood/`, with descriptions in `cat_notes.json`. In the tabby cat photo, keyboard letters and a candy wrapper are blurred out.
- Photo · video uploads and feed deletion need **the backend running**.
- The unread notification count shows only on screens with the bell (not on Welcome · sign-in screens).
- Once the demo booking's **pick-up time has passed**, tasks · check-ins on the sitter screen are blocked (1.3).
- After the rename (2026-10-06), the first visit changes the sign-in session storage key, so you are **signed out once**, and task reminder snoozes reset too. New photos · videos go to the Cloudinary `goldito/` folder (since the Goldito rename on 2026-10-07), and old `pawddy/` · `pawnote/` photos still show.

---

## 5. Bugs · requests

- **Bugs**: file a GitHub issue (title · body in English, per the [CLAUDE.md](../../CLAUDE.md) rules). At least include the **scenario ID · account · what you did · expected · actual · screenshot**.
- **Requests for new scenarios**: add a row to the table below and tell a developer. Once confirmed, it moves to section 3 above.

| Date | Requested by | Feature | Role | Expected result I want to check | Status |

## 6. Update rules

1. In the commit that finishes a task, update the row status in the **section 2 status table** and the **section 3 scenarios** (new IDs if needed) together.
2. If you added automated tests, write the spec name in the "Auto" column, and leave **what automation can't see** (real phone · realtime · design) as 👤.
3. When a person has checked it, write the date and name in the status, like `✅ 10/05 Minsik`. If it fails, ❌ and an issue link.
4. When a new limit comes up, add one line to section 4.
